Use before ranking potencies, calling a compound selective or inactive, or saying what a drug hits or is approved for from chembl_* output. - **chemical-class-fraction-quantification**: Use when you have a list of metabolites identified by NMR (Chenomx) or similar profiling and need to report the chemical-class composition as fractions/percentages of the total detected metabolome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-fraction-quantification))
- **chemical-class-grouping-analysis**: Use when an aligned annotated molecular formula table (from IDSL.UFA) contains many related formulas and the goal is to collapse them into homologous formula-class clusters (e.g., perfluorinated, halogenated, or lipid chain families) rather than treating each peak independently. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-grouping-analysis))
- **chemical-class-mapping**: Use when you have a GNPS molecular network (GML or GraphML format) and wish to annotate network nodes with chemical class labels to support metabolite family interpretation. Use it specifically when you need to overlay chemical classification schemes (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-class-metadata-integration**: Use when you have generated a GNPS molecular network (classical or feature-based workflow) and possess chemical class annotations (from GNPS library matching, ClassyFire, or other structural classifiers) that you wish to propagate onto network nodes and edges to enable chemical family-level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-metadata-integration) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-class-node-mapping**: Use when you have a GNPS mass spectral molecular network (in graphml or cytoscape format) and want to enrich its nodes with chemical class information derived from GNPS public library spectral matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-node-mapping) · 3 copies)
- **chemical-class-novelty-detection**: Use when when you have CANOPUS chemical class predictions for your samples and need to identify which extracts contain chemical classes absent from the literature for their species or genus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-novelty-detection) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-class-novelty-scoring**: Decision support for prioritizing natural extracts whose MS/MS-derived chemical taxonomy (CANOPUS/NPC classes) contains classes absent from literature reports for the same species or genus, as one of four components in Inventa's Priority Score ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-novelty-scoring))
- **chemical-class-ontology-lookup-scoring**: Use when NPLinker has produced candidate BGC–MS/MS spectrum links (e.g., via standardised Metcalf scoring) and you need to filter false positives by matching genome-based and structure-based chemical class ontologies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-ontology-lookup-scoring))
- **chemical-class-propagation-onto-networks**: Use when a GNPS molecular network plus an MS2LDA experiment exist and molecular families lack GNPS spectral library matches, so NAP/DEREPLICATOR annotations and ClassyFire classes can be propagated onto nodes/edges to reach MSI annotation level 3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-propagation-onto-networks))
- **chemical-class-set-comparison-across-taxonomic-levels**: Use when you have a canopus_npc_summary.tsv from a recomputed SIRIUS project space plus taxon-level reported-class tables and want to flag extracts whose predicted chemical classes are novel to the species or genus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-set-comparison-across-taxonomic-levels))
